# Top 100 Medical AI-Ready Datasets 🧬🚀

> **“The World's Leading AI-Ready Medical Data Resource Library.”**  
> **“全球领先的 AI-Ready 医学数据资源库。”**

Welcome to **Top 100 Medical AI-Ready Datasets**! This repository is officially maintained by the [QianfangHub](https://www.qianfanghub.com/) team.

We curate **707 classic, AI-ready medical datasets** — spanning multi-modal imaging, electronic health records, genomics, drug discovery, medical NLP, physiological signals, public health and clinical trials. Every dataset listed here is **AI-ready**: structured, de-identified and annotated for direct model training, research validation and clinical AI development. This repository is **permanently free** for academic labs, young scientists and AI startups.

---

## 🌐 About QianfangHub & AI-Ready Dataset Hub

**[QianfangHub](https://www.qianfanghub.com/)** is a world-leading platform for compliant clinical case dataset curation and full-stack supply.

The **AI-Ready Dataset Hub** at [https://www.qianfanghub.com/ai-ready-dataset/](https://www.qianfanghub.com/ai-ready-dataset) is our flagship collection: **100 hand-picked, globally recognized AI-ready medical datasets**, each with a rich bilingual Wikipedia-style page covering dataset scale, tasks, data types, licensing, publishing institutions and access links.

- 🌐 **AI-Ready Dataset Hub**: [https://www.qianfanghub.com/ai-ready-dataset/](https://www.qianfanghub.com/ai-ready-dataset)
- 🏠 **Official Website**: [https://www.qianfanghub.com/](https://www.qianfanghub.com/)

## 🤝 B2B Custom Medical Dataset Service

Beyond public datasets, QianfangHub addresses industry pain points — insufficient public data scale, missing modalities, and lack of long-term follow-up. Leveraging deep hospital partnerships and a professional medical team, we deliver high-standard, clinically-grade **multi-modal dataset customization** for multinational pharmaceutical R&D and AI leaders.

- **Free Consultation**: medical data experts evaluate your feasibility & compliance plan within 24 hours.
- **Free Samples**: 1–5 customized standard multi-modal samples delivered free for zero-cost blind testing.
- **Commercial Delivery**: industry-first minimum order of 100 entries, ideal for paper publication, model cold-start and early target validation.

👉 **[Submit Your Dataset Request 🚀](https://www.qianfanghub.com/consult)**

## 🤝 B2B 定制化医学数据集服务

除了公开数据集外，针对公开数据规模不足、模态缺失、无长期随访等行业痛点，千方依托深厚的医院底层渠道与专业医学团队，为跨国药企 R&D、AI 大厂提供高标准、临床级的**多模态数据集定制交付**。

- **免费方案咨询**：医学数据专家 24 小时内免费评估数据可行性及合规治理方案。
- **免费样例提供**：免费清洗并交付 1–5 条定制化标准多模态样例数据，支持指定格式零成本盲测。
- **商业定制交付**：行业首创 100 条数据起订门槛，适配课题组论文发表、模型冷启动与药企早期靶点验证。

👉 **[立即提交您的数据集需求 🚀](https://www.qianfanghub.com/consult)**
